- GATE-DEPENDENCY-EXPOSURE: PENDING_FINAL_RECONCILIATION — The delivered build and backend are checked for applicable dependency/advisor findings, and unresolved material findings are documented with exact exposure rather than hidden by passing tests.

- GATE-EXTERNAL-LIMITATIONS: PENDING_FINAL_RECONCILIATION — State exact current provider/configuration/native/external-preview limits with evidence; a candidate hold is not a final accepted hold.

- GATE-NO-FIXABLE-FAILURES: PENDING_FINAL_RECONCILIATION — Remaining ordinary fixable failures must be zero before final acceptance; pending verification cannot be relabeled a pass.
- GATE-EVIDENCE-ACCEPTANCE: PENDING_FINAL_RECONCILIATION — Final report ties each canonical item to implemented state, local checks, actual production checks, visual/technical proof and genuine unresolved reason.
